AUTHORIA - CS PARTICLE-LIGHT EMITTER CENSUS
============================================================
Date: 2026-08-16.  Profile: DEEZ CS NUTZ.  Community Shaders 1.8.0 (AIO 2026-08-09).

WHAT WAS SCANNED
  Every winning LOOSE .nif in the enabled mods (3847 mods + overwrite): 73349 meshes.
  Winner = MO2 priority (lowest modlist.txt index wins, overwrite first). BSA-only meshes were NOT scanned
  (vanilla/DLC archives ship no ENB-light emitters; the vanilla glow billboards inside them only matter if a
  vanilla texture were ever registered, which this census recommends against).

WHAT COUNTS AS AN EMITTER (mirrors CS 1.8.0 LightLimitFix::CheckParticleLights + GetParticleLightConfig)
  BSTriShape with a BSEffectShaderProperty, parent node is a NiBillboardNode,
  ShaderFlags1 has SoftEffect + ZBufferTest, NiAlphaProperty flags == 4109 (0x100D, additive).
  CS then needs Data\ParticleLights\<texture stem>.ini to exist, or the disc is just a sprite.
  CS light radius = disc bound radius x node scale x 0.5.  Colour = alpha-weighted vertex colour x emissive / pi.
  The ini only carries [Light] Cull = true/false. Nothing else is tunable in 1.8.0.

RESULT: 1449 eligible emitters in 865 meshes, 46 texture stems.
  registered (light works today):   433 emitters, 5 stems: black, fxevgnnsmpglow, fxglowenb, glowsoft01_enbl, po3_fullalpha
  NOT registered (sprite only):     1016 emitters, 41 stems

HEADLINE CONCLUSIONS
  1. Do NOT register the vanilla glow textures (glowsoft01, fxglowspotlinearalpha, glowslightflash, cloudtile, darkswirls,
     vaportile01...). Measured: they are overwhelmingly spell / weather / fire VFX. glowsoft01 alone would light 58 Storm
     Lightning bolt meshes at radius 128-768. There is no per-mesh scoping in CS 1.8.0.
  2. Only ONE unregistered stem is both mod-specific and safe: pp_black (CC Arcane Accessories - ENB Light Addon).
  3. Many ENB-light items are "dead by size" under CS even when registered: the disc is tiny (fire salts 0.5, void salts 5,
     Barbas 2, Barenziah box 0, enchanting tables 6, Dawnbreaker 5-8). CS cannot enlarge them. Light Placer can.
  4. Real item-glow gaps worth a Light Placer bulb (or a re-pointed texture copy + ini):
       - Improved Dwemer Glass ENB lights carried by the GDPR patch (6 meshes, 21 discs, r16-108, texture glowsoft01)
       - Winterhold mage statue (r100, texture fxglowspotlinearalpha)
       - Ayleid great welkynd stone static (CC shared, r38-128, texture glowsoft01)
       - Kabu fire salts (dead size), Barenziah jewelry box (dead size), Dawnbreaker (dead size),
         alchemy stations (r5-16), enchanting tables (r6), void salts (r5)
  5. Everything Embers XD / FYX / Lux / Rudy candle related is deliberately left unregistered: those fires and candles
     already carry Light Placer or placed lights; a particle light on top would double them.

NOTES PER STEM
  glowsoft01: VANILLA texture. 58 of 122 emitters are Storm Lightning bolt meshes (radius 128-768). Registering it globally would put huge lights on lightning. DO NOT register. Item cases inside it (Improved Dwemer Glass via GDPR patch, Ayleid welkynd stone, Dawnbreaker, alchemy stations) need Light Placer or a re-pointed texture copy.
  fxglowspotlinearalpha: VANILLA texture. Mostly spell VFX (Requiem Magic Redone, VFX Edits) plus giant campfire glow at radius 458 (would double Embers/Lux). DO NOT register. Item cases: Winterhold mage statue (r100), dwemer dynamo/aetherium cores (r9-40).
  glowslightflash: VANILLA texture. Spell VFX, forge markers (double with Embers forge lights), campfire LODs. DO NOT register.
  pp_black: Mod-specific black sprite (CC Arcane Accessories - ENB Light Addon + one DynDOLOD nohavok fire pillar). SAFE to register with Cull = true. Small radii (8-25) except the fire pillar (126).
  white: Generic name but only 3 eligible emitters (Praedy apocrypha light posts, which are ALSO lit by a registered black disc; Frost VFX inv art). Registering gains nothing.
  krglowtile: Crystalline Soul Gems filled-gem glow discs (r32). Already lit by Authoria_Glow_SoulGems.json (Light Placer). Registering would double. Leave.
  enb_particle_glow: GDPR dwemer pipes + GDPR smelter. Disc inside the metal, r16-108. Handled by Authoria_Glow_DwemerPipes.json (Light Placer). Do NOT register (would cull the visible glow and stack a tiny light).
  glowsoft01_enbl_half: Kabu Fire Salts orange glow. Disc radius 0.5 -> CS light would be 0.5 units. Registering is useless; Light Placer only.
  enb_glow: GDPR-LOTD dwemer compass / load screen art, r2-4. Dead size. Ignore.
